# Benchmark Methodology

**Category**: `benchmark_methodology`
**Confidence**: inferred
**Last compiled**: 2026-09-25 (System One and evolutionary-agents intake); previous: 2026-09-25 (publication metric/era/provenance boundaries and prospective evidence contracts); earlier: 2026-09-24 (speech-lane wrap-up compile: recording rules from the opencode-seat metrics and the conversation-stack source row: name the unit, define the quantity, absent is not zero, a failed measurement is not zero cost, per-metric direction, reference harness is not a serving claim); earlier: 2026-09-24 (wrap-up compile: RTG-16 — a total-outage run pooled as wrong answers manufactured the `real_suite_v1` instability flag, now fixed and re-scored, with the MDE half still open; a pinned `test` extra makes the research scorer suites runnable for the first time; the per-arm parse-failure-rate acceptance rule for the TD-21 scorer conversions; an 8-token probe is not a throughput number); earlier: 2026-09-23 (later: ETR-1/NIB2-59 RATIFIED — the operator ruled the digest inside the trust boundary and the amendment landed (MEASUREMENT.md §5, era E17); NIB2-73b closed as a symlink-spelling false alarm, not a registry defect; SW-7 generalises evidence-durability resolution to the git-common-dir main clone, adding a fourth `PRESENT_IN_MAIN_CLONE` verdict distinct from `OK`/`WITHHELD`/`MISSING`); earlier: 2026-09-23 (evening wrap-up compile: ETR-1 ruled — agent-caused (task_failed) rows score 0 INSIDE the quality denominator while infra/scoring failures stay excluded (MEASUREMENT.md clause and era E17 prepared, not applied); EV-6b's cross-family judge check was vacuous on its only production call site and now fails closed and LABELS llm_judge rows, revealing that every one reads same_family on an all-Qwen lineup; a scorer shelling to bare python3 silently scored False; 24 bundle-less E8 tests retired; n=50 chosen by measuring the host, not by assuming n is cheap); earlier: 2026-09-23 (wrap-up compile: KLD gates damage but cannot rank near-baseline quality; flip counts need greedy decoding or a measured flip floor; the P-KLD annex proposals exist as PREPARE-only wording for the operator's OP-38 package; QFS and arXiv 2608.11212 share lineage with our annex and are not independent corroboration; the GLM-5.3-Flash v44 KV-as-weight mislabel as the worked declared-estimand example; a competing-inference gate that asks whether a process did WORK; two instrument-class self-corrections from the DSpark run; a test that always supplies the value under test cannot fail on it); 2026-09-23 (later: `bench_canonical.sh`'s in-window placement sampler never fired on a 483 GiB no-mmap model across five runs, so every canonical run self-downgraded to OBSERVATION — a gate degrading quietly toward the safe verdict; plus AKX-P5a's FORWARD class: structural-only admission with no non-inferiority row fabricated); 2026-09-23 (main-kernel wrap-up: **the token cap is part of the claim** — a quality gate that never recorded its own `max_tokens` inverted a promotion verdict, 0.5650 vs 0.6450 read as an 8-point regression where the converged figures are 0.7550 vs 0.6450; plus the degeneracy precondition for raising a cap, and a residual computed by subtraction understating itself by 0.277 GiB); 2026-09-22 (incremental: v10 promotion gates: max-performance only, scope is command output, contamination filter counted the bench itself, clause the incumbent fails is no gate, R23-61b CIs overlap); earlier: 2026-09-19 (judge-redundancy harness first run: CJ-13/CJ-14 typed 95.8% vs free-form 12.5% with 63/72 parse failures, blind prior 59.7%, agreement on 9 co-resolved criteria only — observation-grade, n=24, one run); 2026-09-18 (backlog-churn-2 sweep: TD-1d re-measured at n=4 — 9.60x, bar not cleared, the whole disagreement in the JSON baseline; the denominator caveat; option (b) a measured loser); 2026-09-17 (wrap-up sweep: the typed-decision measurement design — 6.25%/37.5% contamination, ECE 0.0625/0.267 and the no-gate rule, fan-out 2.2x at 87.5% on a content-anchored catalogue, closed-set tool-arg exactness, receipts/refusals in the belief kernel, and the reviewer-truncation scoring fix); 2026-09-17 (research-intake delta: judge redundancy (CJ-13/14), the per-million-checks cost order (ECR-TD-1), citation snapshot discipline (ECR-TD-2), and the write-side-before-first-run rule (VB-TDP-1); evening: the landing audit: cited evidence must resolve on main, and main's INF-61 port lacked its own fence); earlier 2026-09-17 (afternoon: ETR-4's guard gets the test it never had, including a structural ban on the bare read); earlier 2026-09-17 (later pass: the v7 runner-pin re-seal's scope and equivalence evidence, the harness-null-pool evidence packet for HS-4, the Dream-RSI measurement corrections, the promotion-rule/identity fields, and the PRB-T4 scorer fix reaching orchestrator main; earlier incremental: PRB-T4 shared-scorer defects (stale pool, A–H scorer, first-n loader), CJ-1e GPQA pair unresolved, EV-13b swap gate fail, EV-CONF-2 MTP-placeholder finding, RC-9/E1a false closures rebuilt, reviewer dual-gold/envelope/bias probes); earlier: 2026-09-15 (the eval tower must measure calibration and discrimination, not just accuracy — a confidence stub silently worth 10–15% of every RLVR score, the frozen-v9 logprob read-out defects, cross-family verification falsified as an anti-reward-hacking defence, and the quality lane's chosen-not-measured resolution; earlier: 2026-09-14 (noninf sweep: **absence is a STATE, not a number** — `float(x or 0.0)` on a Pareto axis manufactures a permanent frontier point, and replay corrected the believed blast radius from 231 admitted rows to 0 frontier points before *and* after; **a guard is not deployed until something reads it** (`quality_measured` computed, documented and consulted by nobody, so the only protection was the independent REL-1 floor, and `answer_without_generation` closes the non-blank-zero-token hole); **a report must record the fact its number depends on** — `n_questions` is now the REQUESTED count with a completeness set beside it, and `baseline_pin` stores the comparator as made, at compare time, with a self-announcing legacy fallback (EV-14d/EV-14e closed); **a fence's clearing path must not run through the write it blocks**, or two fail-closed fences latch each other; **a stopping rule destroys its own evidence**, so the counterfactual is captured at stop time or never; **the subset IS the metric** (90 of 456 Tulving questions scored into a metric they are not part of; Kendall tau with no coverage requirement paid 1.0 for answering 22%); a floor's `unit` and `n` are DERIVED from the harness and checked before any launch, never annotated after the fact; and **the measurement pin is not the champion** — the canonical headline's binary was build 10303, not the champion's 10301, an unstated instrumentation delta rather than a wrong lineage, and only digestible because the scratch tree had not yet been cleaned; plus, from the parallel autokernel session, **a progress curve anchored on the campaign's own champion measures accumulation, not capability** (12 isolated production-anchored curves, a release is a dated zero-percent epoch) and **a critic demanding payoff proof before authoring livelocked 8 batches / 24 critic calls to zero measurements**); earlier: 2026-09-14 (incremental: a harness-search "win" describes the infrastructure regime until proven otherwise — a public 28-candidate small-model search was homogeneous within regime and its one clear shift coincided with provider rate limits disappearing; AP-55/AP-56 promotion gates, the DCP-12 flip-floor power prerequisite, timeout censoring, grader isolation, and shipped scorers that are not their paper's metric); earlier: 2026-09-08 (late, operator correction): **an absolute throughput COMPARES across a condition boundary; a FLOOR does not** — t/s is a physical quantity, so pre-change headlines are the reference point and improvement is a genuine gain while a REGRESSION is the signal to investigate the change; only ATTRIBUTION (the ordinary two-conditions-at-once confound) and FLOORS (dispersion, which gates A/B decisions and mis-gates in BOTH directions when stale) need care, and conflating the three is what produced the over-claim that a BIOS era boundary voids every number before it; earlier: 2026-09-08 (evening): **the recipe FORMAT, not the kernels, is the binding constraint on what a campaign can ask** — three expressiveness gaps found and fixed in ONE day (env var `b5f58b74`, identity hash `4952e95e`, self-drafting model `c3e362a1`), each blocking a measurement outright and each found by tripping over it mid-measurement; and **a dispersion that does not tighten when n doubles is a property, not a small sample** (2.350% at n=3 → 2.696% at n=6 on the same configuration); earlier: 2026-09-08 (pm, R23-58): a bench-surface number is not a serving number (tg128 understated the real serving rate ~2.5x, 31.0 vs 79.25 tok/s); a null is admissible only with its POWER stated and its knob PROVEN to have fired (48/48 positive-control readbacks); and a floor from n=10 on an extreme order statistic cannot gate — recalibrate at n>=24 and record n plus a CI; earlier: 2026-09-08 pm (a floor must state its UNIT — arm sd 0.501% vs process-launch sd 2.793%, the 1200-fold sizing error; the champion arm is unstable ~12% across process launches while the pristine control reproduces, so a headline needs >=N independent launches with a session-unit CI; a pre-registered sign test fixes CANNOT TELL before the looks; a candidate confound measured flat is retracted to non-confound; earlier: 2026-09-08 (CJ-8/9/11/12 conversion status: the twelve converted sites are the worked set, ~94 surveyed call sites remain unconverted with the rows open by design, CJ-11 closed via a sibling function never a widened return, CJ-12 closed under the operator's OPTION-1 wrapper ruling with upstream bytes untouched; RC-11 ratified separate FA/FR reporting with `None`-not-`0.0` for unmeasured sides, and the RC-12 rubric-threshold remedy serialized `rubric_threshold_source` into every rubric row; earlier: 2026-09-07 (three-valued gate verdicts, CJ-8/9/11/12: the same absence-read-as-pass defect found in three independent subsystems in one afternoon -- GPQA gold fabrication, an RL timeout charged as failure, a missing baseline accuracy silently passing a promotion gate; earlier: two screening-loop measurement defects, both generalizable: a noise floor estimated as p95 over subsets of ONE fixed sample cannot exceed that sample's own tail, so the decode floor was low by 0.846 pp at 9 pairs and ~18× at 20 — rebuilt by bootstrap from a three-condition A/A campaign to 2.422%/2.021%/1.188% at 5/9/20 pairs, with `pp512` still UNCALIBRATED; and a static v9 anchor against an accumulating candidate tree made every effect cumulative rather than marginal, committing a −2.864% regression as a "+1.846% keep" — an anchor must advance with the champion, and when per-commit attribution is lost the correct fallback is a block audit of the whole range; earlier: 2026-08-25 TU-DTAP-1 landed — the reviewed Apache-2.0 DTAP subset is now a disposable local runner with typed failure outcomes, immutable SHA-256 trace replay and target-disjoint attack payloads (66 tests, zero inference), and TU-DTAP-2 is filed for the live-model half; the root L5.self_optimizing_loop readiness criterion closed 2026-08-25 via the vidya-loop detector (queue 13→6, passive-pickup guardrail test-pinned), and the same batch surfaced a no-inference gate that had been failing since 2026-08-03 on a PII fixture/allowlist drift; earlier: 2026-08-23 evening hygiene sweep: the last pre-B7 scorer divergence closed by DELEGATION, not porting — the research-repo `debug_scorer.py` (10/10 defect classes, off routing path) is now a B7 delegation shim with an era stamp, so research benchmarks scored with it inherit eval-tower B7 semantics instead of remaining a diverged duplicate; earlier: Annex D ratified — `P-PARITY-1` and `P-NONDET-1`, the repo's FIRST protocols of any kind for output identity; every parity check until now was ad hoc. Both are STAGED, not ratified, and neither has yet been exercised. Every load-bearing clause traces to a measured false clear: n ≥ 5 prompts because a 1-prompt check false-clears near 50%, a fresh process per phase because `cache_prompt=false` is not a substitute, per-prompt PASS/FAIL with the first-differing generation-token index and NEVER an aggregate, an f16-KV confound control, and per-arm kernel-route capture. `P-NONDET-1` answers the prior question — a configuration that is not bit-identical with itself cannot support any parity, regression or A/B claim — and a one-shape-per-fresh-process harness is structurally blind to it. `llama-bench` is now a formally excluded correctness instrument; previously 2026-08-22: log retention bounds the evidence window — a nine-day llama-server log hole made an upstream correctness disclosure unanswerable from retained evidence, the clean frontdoor log is a negative only inside its window, and the `empty_generation` detector's silence counts only because its 30 s threshold is provably exceeded by the cold-full-prefill mechanism; previously 2026-08-21 evening: Shape C empirical on the MI210 and the omission-class split) (evening hygiene sweep: the last pre-B7 scorer divergence closed by DELEGATION, not porting — the research-repo `debug_scorer.py` (10/10 defect classes, off routing path) is now a B7 delegation shim with an era stamp, so research benchmarks scored with it inherit eval-tower B7 semantics instead of remaining a diverged duplicate; earlier: Annex D ratified — `P-PARITY-1` and `P-NONDET-1`, the repo's FIRST protocols of any kind for output identity; every parity check until now was ad hoc. ## Compiled Update — 2026-09-25: publication must preserve the metric, era, and evidence boundary

**Confidence: verified.** The published matrix decisions, release receipt, frozen release procedure, and
prospective evidence contracts are committed and mutually consistent.

The AMD Lab corpus review found several numbers that were individually real but not interchangeable. The
exact-v10 Qwen3.6 GPU record has a best observed cell of 104.8 tok/s and a separate 229.6 tok/s wall-time
aggregate at np8. Remembered 112.68 and 310.96 tok/s results were found in the corpus, but belong to the
pre-BIOS `ef81196d5` era; no equivalent exact-v10 maximum sweep was located. The publication kept those
out of the current matrix instead of relabelling them. It also kept generation cap `L` distinct from
prompt context, failed or guarded cells missing rather than zero, summed-request decode distinct from
wall-time aggregate throughput, text-only VL scope visible, and speech first-packet time distinct from
playback latency. Presentation can change layout; it cannot change the measurand, denominator, era, or
absence state to fill a visual gap.

The same rule now applies to model-card quantization labels: each card names the
weight quant separately from the serving recipe, and the opened workspace repeats
both. Q8_0, Q4_K_M, F16, and the TTS talker-versus-codec split were verified from
their source evidence rather than inferred from a product label.

Distribution identity is part of reproducibility. The v10 release receipt pins the immutable tag to source
commit `ffc1bac82`, binary version 10303, and per-asset hashes, and records successful fresh extraction.
It also records that the first archives omitted the store's native `PROVENANCE.md` and `SHA256SUMS`. The
release remains immutable; future releases must carry those files or a clearly derived archive manifest.
The runbook therefore builds public archives from the versioned store target, executes the extracted
binaries, verifies backend linkage, and requires a new release event instead of asset replacement or
retagging.

The prospective CPU/gfx90a quantization work applies the same principle before measurement begins.
Measurement and verifier records are separate native source kinds with stable locators, producer and
artifact identities, self-hashes, and explicit experimental authority. Strict readers accept only
post-hook records, project them into the shared `ClaimTuple`, and leave grading to
`claim_tuple.grade()`; historical rows are not reconstructed. Evidence-processing hooks sit inside this
boundary too: the PII hook's rewrite of fourteen `perf` period cells means those committed bundle copies
cannot support byte-identity claims unless they are labelled as derived and retain a digest path to the
original.

## Compiled Update — 2026-09-24 (speech-lane wrap-up): six recording rules for efficiency and voice-turn rows

**Confidence: inferred.** These rules are distilled from two freshly filed sources: the opencode-seat per-call
metrics and the conversation-stack write-side row. Neither has produced a gated measurement yet.

1. **Name the unit.** An opencode *step* (one assistant message) and an orchestrator REPL *turn* are different
   units. A row records which one it counts, and a cross-harness comparison never equates them.
2. **Define the quantity once.** "Decoded" means output tokens summed over steps, **reasoning included**. A second
   harness that counts `tokens_generated` has to say which quantity it means, and keep the two convertible.
3. **Absent is not zero.** When the instrument cannot see a field, the row says *unknown*. `opencode export` has
   no per-tool latency, per-step wall time or compaction step, so none of them can be written as 0.
4. **A failed measurement is not a zero-cost call.** A row carrying `metrics_error` counts as a missing
   measurement, never as a call that cost nothing.
5. **Write the direction per metric in a mixed-direction family.** Voice-turn rows mix `lower_better` metrics
   (latency, TTFA, RTF, WER, co-tenant slowdown) with `higher_better` ones (routing precision/recall,
   paralinguistic accuracy). A family-level direction would invert half the verdicts.
6. **A reference-harness number is never a serving claim.** PyTorch/vLLM reference numbers are measurement-only
   for the eventual llama.cpp port. Co-tenant state sampled during the row is part of the measurand.

Details are in [agent-architecture](agent-architecture.md) (the per-call row) and
[knowledge-management](knowledge-management.md) (VB-SPEECH-CONV-1, VB-AK-METRICS-1).

## Compiled Update — 2026-09-24 (wrap-up compile): a total-outage run pooled as wrong answers manufactured a suite-instability flag, and a test suite nobody could run was reported as passing

**Confidence: verified** — both findings are landed commits with tests and a re-score on the real ledgers.

### Key findings

- **The `real_suite_v1` "run instability" was an outage, not a scorer defect.** The 0/50 vs 35/50 result
  that had blocked OP-1 since July came from `real_suite_v1_eval_20260706T192007Z`, which is a
  **total-outage run**: 50/50 rows carry `error=True` (12 `Backend unavailable (circuit open): :8070`,
  38 no-progress-nudge escalations, `eval_concurrency=3`). `eval_suite_discriminability.py` pooled those
  errors **as wrong answers** against the clean `…20260707T013009Z` run. Not the scorer, not the substring
  matcher, not the seed. Fixed in orch `8a829233`: error rows leave the flip vectors, error-dominated runs
  (`error_rate ≥ 0.5`, `--error-dominated-gate`) leave run-spread and are reported as
  `error_dominated_runs_excluded` / `run_stability_unmeasurable`, and `pass_rate`/MDE are unchanged (ETR-1
  scoring). Re-scored on the two real ledgers the `run_unstable, brittle` flags **clear**, run_spread
  0.70 → 0.0, MDE 0.267 unchanged. 25 tests pass.
  ([loops-and-dashboards-audit](../handoffs/active/loops-and-dashboards-audit-2026-07-05.md) RTG-16;
  [2026-09-24 non-inference Tier-2](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-24-noninf-tier2.md))
- **The generalisable rule: an instrument that cannot see agent-caused failure will attribute it to the
  subject.** A discriminability statistic computed over a denominator that silently contains outage rows
  reports a property of the infrastructure as a property of the suite. This sits beside the standing rule
  that a parse failure scored as a wrong answer is a *scoring artifact*, not a model result — both are the
  same error of attribution.
- **Only half of the discriminability problem is closed.** With n=50 the MDE is **0.267**, still above the
  0.15 target; de-saturating requires a larger `real_suite_v1` run, i.e. inference, and stays with the
  eval-tower owner. Any conclusion drawn from the suite's power before that run is premature.
  ([loops-and-dashboards-audit](../handoffs/active/loops-and-dashboards-audit-2026-07-05.md) RTG-16)
- **Reports produced before the fix are partially inadmissible.** Filed as a belief-kernel caution:
  discriminability reports older than orch `8a829233` must have their `run_unstable` / `brittle` /
  flip_rate claims **refused** wherever an input run was error-dominated; their MDE and pass_rate stay
  admissible. That is a per-field admissibility split, not a blanket retraction.
  ([vidya-belief-substrate-program](../handoffs/active/vidya-belief-substrate-program.md) VB-EVALDISC-1)
- **"Tests pass" was not reproducible for the research repo's scorer suites, and now is.** Found
  2026-08-12: `test_score_with_claude.py` uses pytest fixtures with no stdlib fallback, but **no pytest
  existed** in system python, `.venv-exec`, or user site — a stale `__pycache__` pyc proved pytest 9.1.1
  had run once and vanished, and both auditor lanes had verified through ad-hoc scratch venvs. Closed
  2026-09-24 (research `6cf4a076`..`cbb12fc7`): a `test` extra pins `pytest==9.1.1`, the README installs it
  into a gitignored `.venv-test` (**never** `.venv-exec`), and the four scorer suites run — 52 passed.
  A main-thread review corrected the report twice: the README's claim that the whole `scripts/benchmark/`
  glob runs is false (4 collection errors remain, out of this scope), and the per-file counts were wrong
  though the total was right.
  ([scoring-infra-standardization](../handoffs/active/scoring-infra-standardization.md) 1d;
  [2026-09-24 non-inference Tier-2](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-24-noninf-tier2.md))
- **The parse-failure warrant is now quantified across the stack, and it sets an acceptance rule.** The
  JSON-consumer audit records that 9 of 36 free-text consumers **score a parse failure as a wrong answer**,
  and the standing measured warrant is the closed-set 18/18 vs free-form 6/18 result (the free-form
  failures being parse/schema failures) plus a 4.1% parse-failure rate over 1,366 `review_decision` events.
  Acceptance for the scorer conversions is therefore stated up front: **report the per-arm parse-failure
  rate beside every accuracy, before and after** — the warrant being the verbose arm's 15% false
  parse-failures vs 0% and the gpqa 43.4% → 53.0% re-score.
  ([typed-decision-plane](../handoffs/active/typed-decision-plane.md) TD-21, TD-21.9–21.14)
- **A throughput number taken from an 8-token probe is not a throughput number.** The AutoKernel planner
  server was reported at "17 t/s"; measured properly it decodes 53 t/s. Self-corrected in-session. Short
  probes measure the launch and prefill path, not steady-state decode.
  ([2026-09-24 main-dsv41](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-24-main-dsv41.md))

### Open questions

- Four `scripts/benchmark/` collection errors remain outside the pinned scorer scope
  (`test_aa_omniscience_manifest`, `test_m12_review_fixes`, `test_rope_position_probe`,

## Compiled Update — 2026-09-23 (evening wrap-up compile): an agent-caused failure now scores 0 inside the denominator, the cross-family judge check stops being vacuous, and a scorer that shelled to bare `python3` scored False in silence

**Confidence: verified** for the code changes and their tests. The MEASUREMENT.md clause and era row that accompany the ETR-1 ruling are **prepared, not applied** — the measurement trust boundary is human-amendment-only and the operator has not yet run the ratify script.

### Key findings

- **ETR-1 ruled: `task_failed` rows score 0 inside the quality denominator; infra and scoring failures stay excluded.** `eval_tower.py:5129` filtered the denominator with `not r.error`, so an agent/config-caused failure and a platform-caused failure were treated **identically** — both simply left the denominator, and the distinction survived only into telemetry and `reliability`. The consequence that made it a ruling: a config that fails often could score higher than one that answers everything. Code: orchestrator `44d0d4a0` + `927380a5` — the aggregate now carries `quality_denominator_policy` and `task_failed_count`, and EV-11 per-role accuracy uses the same helper. Era row **E17** plus the MEASUREMENT.md §5 clause ship in `scripts/operator/ratify_trust_boundary_and_etr1_20260923.sh`, which only the operator runs. ([eval-tower-loop-robustness-audit-2026-07-20](../handoffs/active/eval-tower-loop-robustness-audit-2026-07-20.md) ETR-1, [2026-09-23 progress](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-23.md))
- **A permissive default made the cross-family judge check vacuous at its only production call site.** eval_tower passed a **role** name (e.g. `architect_general`) as `verifier_model`; no `VERIFICATION_FAMILIES` pattern matches a role; the family resolved unknown; the permissive default then returned True for every generator. The `llm_judge` path had no family check at all. Fix (orchestrator `21cb7756`, 39 tests): roles resolve to served models via the lean registry, an unknown family **fails closed** as `unverified`, gpt-oss/glm/kimi/nemotron/minimax patterns added, the rubric-judge path **gates** and the `llm_judge` path **labels** each row (`judge_independence`, `judge_independence_counts`) rather than refusing. **The label is the finding**: with an all-Qwen lineup, every `llm_judge` row now reads `same_family`. A check that cannot fail is not a check, and the honest remedy here was to make the condition visible rather than to block the only judges we have. ([eval-tower-verification](../handoffs/active/eval-tower-verification.md) EV-6b)
- **A scorer that shells to bare `python3` scores False without saying why.** `scripts/benchmark/debug_scorer.py` ran code tasks with `python3` from PATH, so outside the venv every pandas-dependent task silently scored False — the same shape as OBS-12. Both code-execution sites now use `sys.executable`, with an AST guard test (orchestrator `46e79e27`). ([non-inference-backlog](../handoffs/active/non-inference-backlog.md) NIB2-74)
- **Skipped tests are not coverage, and retiring them is a deliberate act.** The 12 E8 unit tests whose sealed staging bundles had left the host were `skipif`-skipped. Operator ruled RETIRE (per OP-19): 24 bundle-dependent tests removed, 28 skips → 0, the remaining 386 pass (orchestrator `5fa290be`). The alternative readings — restore the bundles or rebuild equivalent fixtures — were rejected by the owning campaign rather than left implicit. ([non-inference-backlog](../handoffs/active/non-inference-backlog.md) NIB2-75)
- **An A/A calibration phase is measuring the floor, not wasting time.** The AutoKernel loop opens with 48 matched A/A launches (~2 h, each reloading 519 GB) to measure **relaunch dispersion** — the noise every candidate pays, because a new binary is a new process. Removing it would mean keeping weights in RAM via mmap, which changes NUMA placement and therefore changes what is measured. ([2026-09-23 main-dsv41](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-23-main-dsv41.md))
- **CJ-1d retained the full pinned 198-question set after measuring the actual tradeoff.** The canonical plan estimates at most 5.3 MI210 GPU-hours per model; n=198 gives a ±5.5-point CI at 81% versus ±7.7 points at n=100, while paired sign-test power for a 7-point gap at 18% discordance is only 0.59 even at n=198. The former 2026-08-25 progress link and its n=50/host-throughput rationale were not traceable to a surviving source and conflict with the completed full-set plan, so they are retired. ([canonical-judge-suite-revamp](../handoffs/active/canonical-judge-suite-revamp.md) CJ-1d)

### Open questions

- The ETR-1 / trust-boundary amendment is awaiting the operator's `--apply`; until then the code and the constitution disagree about the denominator policy.
- NIB2-73b — the check is still red on the `architect_critic` / `qwen38_flash_next` known gaps.
- EV-6b now labels rather than gates the `llm_judge` path. What licenses a quality claim when every judge row reads `same_family`?

## Compiled Update — 2026-09-23 (wrap-up compile): a fidelity metric cannot rank quality, a corroborating source must be independent, and an estimand must be declared

**Confidence: verified** for our own gate changes, code anchors and measured gate allowance. **External** for the published fidelity-vs-quality evidence: dive-verified, not reproduced. The P-KLD annex items are **proposals only**. The measurement trust boundary is human-amendment-only, and nothing here amends it.

### Key findings

- **KLD gates damage; it cannot rank near-baseline quality.** Over whole quant cohorts, KL(BF16‖quant) tracks task quality (Spearman ρ −0.72 / −0.86). Among quants near the baseline it does not (ρ +0.00 at n=17, and −0.24), and that holds across 14 per-token variants, including PPL ratio and top-1 agreement (intake-1510#01, intake-1510#02). As a per-prompt picker it scored 42.3-49.4%, which is chance (intake-1510#04). The operative rule: a KLD/PPL/top-1 ordering among candidates that all pass a non-inferiority gate is a *fidelity* ordering. Selecting among them needs task evidence (intake-1519#05). Our own trellis port gates were rewritten this way; see [quantization](quantization.md).
- **Task evidence means paired per-item flips, read against a flip floor.** Report C→I and I→C separately with the net direction, and count leapfrogs too. Measure greedily, or read the count against an R-vs-R flip floor. Two *sampled* 16-bit runs differ by 23.5% flips, so a sampled flip count without a floor is mostly sampling noise (intake-1519#00, intake-1519#record). The same paper's MT-Bench "flips predict downstream quality" headline was overturned on recount, a pairing error (intake-1519#record). Cite that entry by claim.
- **The P-KLD annex proposals are prepared, not adopted.** [p-kld-annex-proposals-20260923](../docs/research-intake/p-kld-annex-proposals-20260923.md) drafts wording from nine dived sources, keyed to annex sections. None conflicts with the staged annex, which is untouched (`artifacts/operator/staged/p-kld-annex-20260915.md`). The load-bearing proposals:
  - **V6/V8:** a fidelity ordering is never a quality ordering, and no secondary statistic converts one into the other (1510.D1).
  - **V5/V6:** a bit-identical R×R repeat is a *reproduction*, not a floor (1507.D1). Floor LEVEL is distinguished from capture VARIABILITY (1511.D2). A floor from another compute plane is never subtracted (1507.D4).
  - **V4:** R×Q is an interchange intervention, not the natural compute-only effect (1520.D1). Route-set disagreement is defined as per-layer mean top-k Jaccard beside its R×R floor (1524.D2).
  - **V1:** teacher provenance — a BF16-stored reference of an FP8/FP4-native release is `dequantized_from_quant` unless audited (1507.D2).
  - **V9:** a mandatory treatment field separating weight codec, KV-cache type and activation scheme (1507.D3, 1508.D2).

  Adoption is the operator's call through the OP-38 ratify package (R23-47). A session may only prepare it. ([autokernel-rebuild-program](../handoffs/active/autokernel-rebuild-program.md) R23-47)
- **Two "corroborating" sources are not independent of our annex.** QFS (quant-fidelity-suite) is a sibling implementation of the same community rtx6kpro protocol that our staged annex distils (intake-1507#09). arXiv 2608.11212 is upstream of the annex's V4 wording, because rtx6kpro cites it (intake-1520#record). Agreement between them and our annex is shared lineage, not replication.
- **The worked example of an undeclared estimand is a KV treatment reported as a weight format.** In a public GLM-5.3-Flash KLD table, the "NVFP4 0.0605" and "same-stack FP8 0.0246" rows read as weight formats. Per the quantizer's own receipts (the "v44" runs), they are EXL3-4bpw *weights* served with an NVFP4 or FP8 *KV cache*. They are single-window rows (2,047 positions), mixed into a 51,175-position panel (intake-1508#00, intake-1508#01, intake-1508#05). The mislabel was never corrected in-thread, and it propagated into QFS's comparability registry (intake-1507#06). A treatment field that names the weight codec, KV type and activation scheme separately would have made it impossible to write. That is why V9 proposes one.
- **Teacher provenance is now a gate step for DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash.** DS41-T3 audits whether its reference is a dequantized upcast before any KLD/divergence claim, because a "BF16" release can sit on the FP8 grid (intake-1508#04). ([deepseek-v41-flash-evaluation](../handoffs/active/deepseek-v41-flash-evaluation.md))
- **External expectation for the spec-OFF confidence arm, and what a bad result means.** Published mean-token-prob AUROC on GSM8K is 0.690 (0.662-0.723 across 8 SLMs), and about 0.72 across GSM8K / MultiArith / SVAMP. It is near-random on AQuA (intake-1525#02). A spec-OFF result near or below 0.5 therefore points at the instrument first. The probe's fail-closed checks (draft_n > 0, placeholder tokens) are what detect a speculative server leaking into it. ([autopilot-decision-plane-audit](../handoffs/active/autopilot-decision-plane-audit-2026-07-22.md))
- **A competing-inference gate should ask whether a process did WORK, not whether it exists.** DS41-C2b-gate brackets each measured span with cumulative `utime+stime` reads for the unowned inference-like processes. The counters are monotone, so a burst between samples cannot hide. The allowance is pinned to measurement. Over a 279 s idle observation, all 13 resident servers together accrued ≤ 0.06 core-seconds. The gate's 0.5 core-s + 0.02 cores/s sits ~100x above idle and ~46x below one busy core. It turns "the planner never runs during measurement" into a checked invariant. ([deepseek-v41-flash-evaluation](../handoffs/active/deepseek-v41-flash-evaluation.md) DS41-C2b-gate)
- **A binary must identify the commit it was built from.** The DS41-C2c dry run first rebuilt the binary from the committed tree, so it self-identifies as `version 10310 (ad932bbd9)` on a clean tree. It had previously reported `7c18bb8c1` from an uncommitted build, which is the INF-70 C9 shape. ([deepseek-v41-flash-evaluation](../handoffs/active/deepseek-v41-flash-evaluation.md) DS41-C2c)
- **Two instrument-class self-corrections from the DSpark run, both caught before they hardened.**
  - A llama-bench tg128 (13.18) was compared against a serving run (6.01). That comparison is refused, not caveated; the same-class control is 8.21-11.43.
  - Two prompts that returned one token and then EOS in every arm were read as "low-entropy, no divergence". The model ships no chat template and the prompts were instruction-phrased, so their silence proved nothing.

  ([2026-09-23 main-dsv41](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-23-main-dsv41.md))
- **A test suite that always supplies the value under test cannot fail on it.** 56 green assertions never caught the push-lock directory defect (SSU-F9). Every shell case pinned the location with `EPYC_PUSH_LOCK_DIR`, so none exercised the default derivation. The mirror-image proof came from SSU-F5: a pytest plugin relocates the worker lane a second time. 34 of 35 tests follow the move with no edit, which shows the expectations are *derived* rather than repinned. The one failure is the deliberate pin, failing loudly at a production defect rather than a test one. ([model-stack-single-source-update-pipeline](../handoffs/active/model-stack-single-source-update-pipeline.md))

### Open questions

- Will the operator fold the P-KLD proposals into OP-38, or reject them? Until then the staged annex governs, and the refinements are advisory.
- The spec-OFF confidence arm has not run. Does it land inside the published 0.66-0.72 band on our stack?
- Does DS41-C2b-gate's work-based allowance hold under the planner's real load pattern, or only in the idle observation it was pinned on?

## Compiled Update — 2026-09-23 (later): a placement gate that never fired on the one model it was written for, and a lever admitted with no non-inferiority row

**Confidence: verified** — the sampler defect was found by comparing five canonical runs that produced
no `placement.log` against a hand-run sampler that did capture the proof.

### Key findings

- **`bench_canonical.sh`'s in-window NUMA placement sampler silently never fired.** Across five runs on
  the 483 GiB no-mmap `deepseek41` model there was no `placement.log` and no `.rc`, so **every canonical
  run self-reported as OBSERVATION** — downgrading itself for a missing proof it had been asked to
  collect. The mechanism: `largest_rss_descendant` walks `/proc/<pid>/task/*/children` down the wrapper
  chain (region-lock → env → taskset → numactl → llama-bench) and never reached the binary. Selecting the
  process by `comm == llama-bench` works, and is what captured the 25.0%-per-node proof.
- **The failure mode is the instructive part: the gate degraded quietly toward the safe verdict.** A
  sampler that cannot find its own subject produces no error and no claim — it produces a *lower grade*,
  which reads like ordinary caution rather than a defect. The case it skipped is precisely the one it was
  written for (a huge no-mmap load where placement is most likely to skew), and the deeper an instrument's
  wrapper chain, the less its own pid-walk can be assumed.
- **A measurement window that misses the phenomenon and a sampler that never opens are the same class of
  failure**, one level down: the first samples outside the event, the second never samples at all.
- **An admission class for levers that cannot be measured yet.** AKX-P5a (`FORWARD`) admits a kernel
  commit **no graph can reach** on structural evidence only, with *no non-inferiority row computed and
  none fabricated* — because the existing gate would otherwise pass it **vacuously** (every workload
  INERT, the TOUCHED set empty). The grammar cost is paid in the record, not in the number: the fold
  states plainly that the headline cannot move, a headline re-measure is labelled a *control with no knob
  fired*, and the admission carries a **mandatory expiry** naming its consumer.
- **`llama-bench` is not a serving rate.** The first DeepSeek-V4.1 sweep is explicitly observation-grade
  (no protocol id, np=1, no drafter, single model); claim grade requires unit=launch, n≥3, the noise floor
  **with its unit**, and a serving-shaped `llama-server` np sweep (DS41-T6b).

### Open questions

- The `bench_canonical.sh` fix lands in `epyc-inference-research`; until it does, canonical runs on large
  no-mmap models keep self-downgrading (DS41-T6c).
- Does any other wrapper-chained instrument select its subject by descendant-walk rather than by `comm`?
  The defect class is not specific to placement sampling.

  sampler defect and its mechanism), DS41-T6/T6b (observation grade, and what claim grade needs).
- [2026-09-23 — main-dsv41](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-23-main-dsv41.md) — the five-run symptom and the

## Compiled Update — 2026-09-23: the token cap is PART OF THE CLAIM — a gate that never recorded its own `max_tokens` inverted a promotion verdict

**The finding, in one line: a capped accuracy is a lower bound on the model, and the cap is not model-neutral.**

`promotion_gates.yaml gates.quality` had never recorded `max_tokens`. The value 64 was carried into the
v9 *and* v10 qualifications by convention — in the head of whoever ran the previous promotion — which is
precisely the failure that file was created to end. At that cap, on the same pinned 200-item manifest,
live-serving `:8074`:

| suite | max_tokens | pooled | untruncated | truncated |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| mmlu_pro | 64 (gate) | 0.5650 | 0.7143 | 46/200 |
| mmlu_pro | 4096 | 0.7500 | 0.7590 | 5/200 |
| **mmlu_pro** | **8192** | **0.7550** | **0.7550** | **0/200** |
| gpqa | 64 (gate) | 0.5436 | 0.6176 | 25/195 |
| gpqa | 4096 | 0.6513 | 0.6492 | 4/195 |
| **gpqa** | **16384** | **0.6513** | **0.6513** | **0/195** |

The retired Qwen3.5-122B recorded **0/200 truncated at the same cap** and scored 0.6450 / 0.5692. So the
gate said the replacement was ~8 points WORSE on mmlu_pro; converged, it is ~11 points BETTER. **The
entire 18.5-point swing was the instrument.** One model answers with a letter; the other derives in the
visible channel (`--no-enable-thinking` set, `reasoning_chars == 0` on every row — not leaked reasoning)
and was cut off mid-derivation, scoring ~0.05 on work that was going fine. A cap that silently favours
terse models is not a quality gate.

**Four rules, each earned:**

1. **A pooled accuracy is NOT reportable while `truncated.n > 0`.** Project the truncated count beside
   every accuracy. `pooled == untruncated` is the definition of converged and the only state in which the
   pooled figure means what a reader will assume it means.
2. **Check for degeneracy BEFORE raising a cap.** "Rerun until truncation hits zero" terminates only if
   the tail is finite. A genuine long derivation converges as the cap rises; a repetition loop never does
   and no cap would ever clear it. The cheap discriminator used here: the most-repeated 60-char shingle
   in each truncated completion (1 in every sampled row → non-degenerate, raise the cap).
3. **A PARTIAL truncation count is not a converged one.** Measured the hard way in this session: cap 4096
   was reported as reaching zero on the strength of the first 56 rows, which happened to contain none.
   The full 200 had five. This is the pooled-accuracy rule one level up — the same error about a
   different quantity.
4. **Historical gate numbers taken without a truncation count are lower bounds of unknown tightness**,
   comparable to each other only to the extent the models are equally terse. They are the record of what
   was gated on; they are not a baseline a future candidate can be measured against without re-running
   both sides.

Landed as `gates.quality.max_tokens` plus a `truncation_audit` sub-gate (`required: true`) carrying the
measurement, the method and the degeneracy caution. The sub-gate nests under `quality` so `package.sh`'s
top-level enumeration does not read it as a seventh required gate — verified rather than assumed.

**A second, unrelated measurement lesson from the same session: a residual computed by SUBTRACTION is a
lower bound on what you did not know to look for.** The MI210 27B VRAM accounting derived its compute
buffer as *everything I cannot otherwise name*, giving 1.802 GiB. Read directly from the kernel at
`LLAMA_ARG_LOG_VERBOSITY=4`, it is 2128.39 MiB = 2.079 GiB. The missing 0.277 GiB is the DRAFT graph's
own `sched_reserve` block, which subtraction folded into noise because nothing told it a second block
existed. Same session, same shape as rule 3: an unmeasured quantity standing in for a measured one.

**Sources**: `handoffs/active/model-stack-single-source-update-pipeline.md` (SSU-F2, SSU-F3, SSU-F6),
`artifacts/operator/vram-gap-27b-20260923.md`, `progress/2026-09/2026-09-23-main-kernel.md`,
`.claude/skills/kernel-promotion/promotion_gates.yaml`, master registry
`roles.architect_critic.performance.general_suite_quality` (epyc-inference-research `58b115ed`).

## Compiled Update — 2026-09-21/22: gates the v10 promotion proved — a scope is command output, a contamination count can be the bench itself, and a clause the incumbent fails is no gate

**Confidence: verified** (each rule was learned from a concrete failure during the v10 promotion).

- **Max-performance numbers only.** An unlabelled table with spec-dec off was shown early in the v10
  session and retracted. Both banked artifacts now carry warning banners. Promotion compares the
  max-performance recipe on v9 against the max-performance recipe on the candidate
  ([kernel promotion log](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-22-kernel-promotion.md)).
- **Never quote a freeze scope from prose.** The runbook sentence "`gpu`, `stt` and `tts` serve none"
  was false by 2026-09-21. The GPU backend resolved four roles, and one of them, Qwen3-VL-30B, is the
  cell that caught the −30% MoE regression. The only admissible scope is `kernel_freeze_scope.py`
  output pasted into the record, reconciled against the `binary_path` count in the priors, and taken
  before the repoint and again after the derived regen, never between them
  ([kernel-freeze-runbook](../docs/reference/kernel-freeze-runbook.md)).
- **A headline-only comparison would have shipped a −30% regression.** Dense models never build the
  CPU-only fused MoE node, so the 27B cell passed. The gate must cover every distinct model shape in
  the backend's scope ([2026-09-21](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-21.md)).
- **A contamination witness can flag the benchmark as its own contaminant.** `c5_reanchor_v2.sh`
  excludes `llama-bench` by name, but `top` truncates COMMAND to `llama-b+`. The bench's own
  4800-6400% CPU was therefore logged as foreign load (`contam_hits` 18 and 61, all false positives).
  Past readings of that counter need re-checking. This is another instance of the vacuous instrument.
- **A gate clause the incumbent fails is not a gate.** Bitwise spec-dec identity fails on v9, which is
  batch-variant by design on all three compute planes. The correctness gate is the MMLU-Pro/GPQA eval
  against the incumbent, with errors 0, and a delta inside the suite's noise band (0.5 pp per item at
  n=200) is not a regression.
- **A prediction recorded before measuring can be falsified, and this one was.** The post-BIOS C5 decode
  gain (+66%) far exceeded the ~20-30% the ledger decomposition implied. That identified the
  decomposition itself as wrong ([hardware optimization](hardware-optimization.md)).
- **Dispersion comparisons use CIs.** R23-61b post-BIOS p95_dev was 7.249% [6.024, 9.371] and pre-BIOS
  was 7.897% [5.520, 11.322]. The intervals overlap, so there is no claimed change, and the unit
  (`process`) is recorded ([rebuild program](../handoffs/active/autokernel-rebuild-program.md)).

### Source References (2026-09-21/22)

- [`progress/2026-09/2026-09-22-kernel-promotion.md`](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-22-kernel-promotion.md) — max-performance ruling
- [`kernel-freeze-runbook.md`](../docs/reference/kernel-freeze-runbook.md) — scope-as-output rule, eval-not-bitwise gate

## Compiled Update — 2026-09-19: the first judge-redundancy run — accuracy and agreement reported apart, and the free-form reader mostly failed to parse

**Confidence: verified** for what the run recorded; **observation-grade** as a finding — n=24 rubric cases, a single run, and the handoff's own instruction is "re-run before citing". Source: [canonical-judge-suite-revamp](../handoffs/active/canonical-judge-suite-revamp.md) → *Research Intake Update — 2026-09-18*.

### Key findings

- **CJ-13's harness ran once, on synthetic ground truth.** 24 rubric cases / 72 criteria with synthetic deterministic ground truth, both readers on Qwen3.6-35B-A3B: **typed judge 95.8% criterion accuracy with 0 failures** vs **free-form LLM judge 12.5% with 63/72 parse failures**; the **blind prior is 59.7%**. Harness `src/typed_decisions/judge_redundancy.py`; receipt under `artifacts/typed_decisions/run_20260918/`. ([canonical-judge-suite-revamp](../handoffs/active/canonical-judge-suite-revamp.md))
- **Agreement is reported apart from accuracy, as CJ-13 requires — and here it is nearly uninformative.** Pairwise agreement is computable only where both readers resolve: 9 of 72 criteria, 100% agreement on those. Agreement over 9 co-resolved criteria says nothing about the 63 the free-form reader never parsed, and "agreement is not accuracy" applies either way. ([canonical-judge-suite-revamp](../handoffs/active/canonical-judge-suite-revamp.md))
- **The blind prior is the floor the readers are judged against (CJ-14's blind-probe discipline).** 59.7% is what a reader gets without looking; the free-form reader's 12.5% sits *below* that floor, and the parse failures account for all of it: 72 − 63 = 9 criteria resolved, and 12.5% of 72 is 9, so on the arithmetic of the recorded figures every criterion the free-form reader did resolve was correct. Read 12.5% as a failure to produce a scorable answer, not as a measure of the free-form judge's reasoning quality (the standing rule that a parse-failure gap is a scoring artifact is recorded further down this page). No adoption decision follows; CJ-GATE remains the operator's, and the CJ-13/CJ-14 boxes are still open in the handoff.

### Open questions

- Does the typed-vs-free-form gap survive a re-run, real (non-synthetic) ground truth, and a free-form reader whose output format is repaired so its failures are judgments rather than parse errors?
- With the free-form reader resolving 9/72, the human-disagreement-cost half of CJ-13 is not yet measurable — what co-resolution rate is needed before agreement means anything?

### Source References (2026-09-19)

- [canonical-judge-suite-revamp.md](../handoffs/active/canonical-judge-suite-revamp.md) — the 2026-09-18 CJ-13/CJ-14 measurement note (typed 95.8%, free-form 12.5% with 63/72 parse failures, blind prior 59.7%, 9-criterion agreement, observation-grade caveat, harness and receipt paths).

## Compiled Update — 2026-09-18: an n=1 acceptance does not survive n=4 — the baseline, not the treatment, moved

**Confidence: verified** — one GPU session on the frozen-v9 HIP server (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-Q8_0, MI210, 4 slots, temp 0), launched and killed by the measuring session, VRAM 59% in all 24 in-run samples. Session record: [`progress/2026-09/2026-09-18-td1d-remeasure.md`](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-18-td1d-remeasure.md); artifacts `artifacts/typed_decisions/run_20260918/`.

### Key findings

- **TD-1d was accepted at 11.98x from one run per arm; four alternated rounds put the same arm at 9.60x, below the ≥10x bar.** Same 24-question catalogue and state hash, one untimed warm pass per arm, then 4 rounds with every arm run before the next round and arm order rotated. `native_id_only` 1.46 ± 0.03 s vs JSON 14.02 ± 1.82 s (11.34–15.45). Agreement is met everywhere (60/64 pooled = 15/16, the same `n01` disagreement as TD-1c) — speed is not. ([typed-decision-plane](../handoffs/active/typed-decision-plane.md), [td1d-remeasure](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-18-td1d-remeasure.md))
- **The disagreement is entirely the baseline.** The two native measurements agree (1.46 s vs 1.6 s); the 09-17 JSON sample (19.3 s) sits **above the maximum of four samples** taken here (15.45 s). Pairing each side's baseline against the other's treatment spans 8.8x–13.2x. A single baseline sample cannot see that spread — **a speed bar needs a repeated, alternated baseline, not just a repeated treatment.** Filed as TD-1d.0 for the accepting session: re-run at n≥4 alternated and keep the tick if it survives, or downgrade it; do not adopt `id_only` as the default on n=1. The tick was left standing with the evidence beside it — the box belongs to the accepting session.
- **The bar never stated its denominator.** Native decides 16/24 (c01–c08 fail closed on multi-token option labels), so the ratio compares a 16-decision run to a 24-decision one. Per decision: 584 ms vs 91 ms = **6.40x**. Neither 11.98x nor 9.60x is like-for-like, and neither passes a per-decision reading. TD-1d.4: handle the 8 multi-token questions natively, or take per-question fallback as a routing policy — not a speed fix.
- **Option (b) parallel reads are a measured loser**: 3 slots 2.67x vs 6.69x on one slot; independent corroboration of the 09-17 `heavy_model` lock note. Two mechanisms recorded: concurrent in-process `llm_call`s are serialized by the cross-process `inference_lock` (parallel wall == serial wall, TD-1d.2), and `qwen35moe` is hybrid-recurrent so prefix reuse is checkpoint-quantized (TD-1d.3) — see [local-inference](local-inference.md).
- **Every arm bypassed `/v1`.** `frontdoor` routes to `/v1/chat/completions`, which forwards neither `grammar` nor `json_schema`: measured 515 free-form tokens and **0/24 decisions**. All arms used a direct `/completion` adapter, and TD-1c's result must have too (TD-1d.1). A typed-decision speed number is not a deployable number until that seam is settled.

### Open questions

- Does the 09-17 acceptance survive its own re-run with an alternated n≥4 baseline (TD-1d.0)?
- Should the bar be restated per decision, with the fail-closed subset named, before any arm is promoted?

### Source References (2026-09-18)

- [typed-decision-plane.md](../handoffs/active/typed-decision-plane.md) — the ⚠️ re-measurement note and TD-1d.0–TD-1d.4.
- [2026-09-18-backlog-churn-2.md](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-18-backlog-churn-2.md) — why the tick was not touched.

## Compiled Update — 2026-09-17 (wrap-up sweep): the typed-decision measurement design — receipts, directions, and a no-gate rule

**Confidence: verified** for our own runs and the write-side plumbing; **external** for referenced intake figures. Local numbers are one run each on the frozen v9 stack unless stated.

### Key findings

- **Write side first, and receipts over claims.** Before the first TD measurement run, VB-TDP-1 wired `scripts/vidya/adapters/typed_decisions_measurement.py`; each study (TD-2 contamination/calibration, TD-3 fan-out, TD-4 tool args) emits one self-hashed ClaimTuple projected through the shared `claim_tuple.grade()`, with `metric_direction` recorded (a write-side gap was found and fixed in the producer). 8 receipts → 28 claims / 84 frames cumulatively; **3 bench reports were refused as non-measurements** — a screening instrument is not a protocol-grade measurement. ([typed-decision-plane](../handoffs/active/typed-decision-plane.md), [vidya-belief-substrate-program](../handoffs/active/vidya-belief-substrate-program.md))
- **Contamination is an order statistic, measured by permutation.** Question-order permutation on one batched call vs singleton calls, 24-question decision set, 48 pairs: flip rate **6.25%** (Qwen3.8-27B, 3/48) vs **37.5%** (LFM2.5-2.6B, 18/48). This is the local counterpart of the external 24.7% order sensitivity (intake-1486) — smaller at our batch size, but the failure mode reproduces. ([typed-decision-plane](../handoffs/active/typed-decision-plane.md))
- **Calibration: ECE and Brier per (model, schema), and the tail is not empty.** 27B: 91.7% accuracy / mean confidence 0.8875 / ECE 0.0625 / Brier 0.078. LFM2.5-2.6B: 50.0% / 0.567 / 0.267 / 0.313. One 27B miss sat at ~0.90 confidence, so a high-confidence wrong answer is possible. **The confidence statistic is not calibrated and no gate may threshold on it**; TD-5 is satisfied only for a first shadow. FW-1 L2's confidence-branching lift is per `(model pin, question catalogue)` on an operator-ratified calibration record, never global — the 27B number says nothing about LFM, and one catalogue's ECE says nothing about another's. ([typed-decision-plane](../handoffs/active/typed-decision-plane.md), [backlog churn](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-17-backlog-churn.md))
- **Fan-out agreement needs a content-anchored catalogue; a content-neutral probe cannot measure it.** TD-3 measured 1.54x (3 batched vs 12 singleton calls, 100% agreement, small state) and 3.22x long-context (three 40k-char states) — but the long-context catalogue was deliberately content-neutral, so its agreement number is uninterpretable. TD-3b re-ran with the real 24-question catalogue: **87.5% agreement (21/24) at 2.2x** (17.2 s batched vs 37.9 s singleton), consistent with the 6.25% order-flip rate. Design rule: an agreement metric needs anchored answers; a speed metric does not. ([typed-decision-plane](../handoffs/active/typed-decision-plane.md))
- **A speed claim needs its parity arm and its cue named.** TD-1b's 18.9x pre-fix was not semantically equivalent (agreement 11/16, native 68.8% vs JSON 91.7% on the overlap); only after TD-1c fixed prompt cueing did 15/16 parity hold at 2.08x; TD-1d recovered **11.98x at 15/16** with the id-only cue (full 2.66x, short 6.0x). Report the cue style with the multiplier, and never promote a native-arm speed past its agreement check. ([typed-decision-plane](../handoffs/active/typed-decision-plane.md))
- **A parse failure is a scoring artifact, and the closed-set arm wins on it.** TD-4's free-form tool-argument arm failed 12/18 — parse/schema failures, not wrong values — against closed-set 18/18 exact (per-argument exact 66 vs 24). Score the failure classes separately or the number reads as a capability gap. ([typed-decision-plane](../handoffs/active/typed-decision-plane.md))
- **Screening instruments are refused a belief-kernel carrier by design.** The cue sweep and bench reports were explicitly declined: the strict reader refuses them, and revisiting needs a ratified `P-TDP` protocol. ([typed-decision-plane](../handoffs/active/typed-decision-plane.md))
- **Reviewer truncation was a latent scoring bias on the reward path; fixed 2026-09-17** (orchestrator `9a7181f2`). `ArchitectReviewService.condense_output()` replaces the 500-char prefix cut (`review_service.py`, feeding `all_approved` → memrl reward) with head 60% + tail 40% + an explicit elision marker (config `review_output_max_chars`, default 4000). Same commit deprecated (not removed) the dead `ChatRequest.tools` fields. ([scoring-infra-standardization](../handoffs/active/scoring-infra-standardization.md), [backlog churn](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-17-backlog-churn.md))
- **The same write-side-before-first-run rule is now standing for GUI-authored workflows (VB-FW-1).** One ClaimTuple per run carries the workflow-document hash, executed node types, each fuzzy node's model pin and question catalogue, and per-gate outcomes with rejection destinations; confidence is recorded and never read by an edge. Filed at design time, not at first run. ([fuzzy-workflow-authoring-gui](../handoffs/active/fuzzy-workflow-authoring-gui.md), [vidya-belief-substrate-program](../handoffs/active/vidya-belief-substrate-program.md))

### Open questions

- Does the id-only cue's 11.98x reproduce on a second run/model, or is it a single-run artifact?
- Does contamination scale with batch size above 24 questions?
- Should a `native_cue_sweep` study get a belief-kernel carrier under a ratified `P-TDP`, or stay a screening instrument?

## Compiled Update — 2026-09-17 (research-intake delta: judge redundancy, cost anchors, and citation snapshot discipline)

**Confidence: verified** for the filings and the session gates; **external** for the intake cost/quality figures (dive-verified list-rate estimates — not measurements of this deployment).

Three eval-consumer disciplines arrive from the same intake batch: a redundancy arm for new judges, a cost-order anchor for judge-reader selection, and a capture rule for external citations.

### Key findings

- **CJ-13 — two-cheap-readers redundancy arm.** Pair a typed judge with a local LLM judge; report pairwise agreement and human-disagreement cost per the intake-1475/1486 methodology; label the result "agreement is not accuracy". No adoption decision — CJ-GATE remains the operator's. ([canonical-judge-suite-revamp](../handoffs/active/canonical-judge-suite-revamp.md))
- **CJ-14 — the dinostomp measurement discipline for new judge instruments**: hashed manifests/run records, a blind probe as the lower bound, pre-registered per-item distributions (intake-1490). The motivating audit is direct: the reference implementation's shipped shuffled-context control was defective, and the informed-vs-blind lift was ~25 points only after correction.
- **ECR-TD-1 — cost-order anchor + same-rubric local comparison.** The per-million-checks cost order at list rates is Jev ~$160, DeepSeek Flash $260, Gemini $1,600, Astra $18,700, Fable 5.1 $33,000 (intake-1475). Before assuming a typed judge is the cheapest adequate reader, run one same-rubric local cheap-judge arm. ([eval-benchmark-cost-reduction](../handoffs/active/eval-benchmark-cost-reduction.md))
- **ECR-TD-2 — external dashboard citations must be snapshot-captured** (payload hash or rendered snapshot) before entering a claim tuple: the "3–329 s" baseline is not derivable from its cited live page (intake-1492#record).
- **VB-TDP-1 — the write side lands before the first measurement run.** One self-hashed ClaimTuple per TD-2/TD-3/PAW-3 run at the source table; "a tuple invented on read cannot gate a decision." ([vidya-belief-substrate-program](../handoffs/active/vidya-belief-substrate-program.md))
- **The quality figures these instruments will adjudicate stay unresolved.** An independent rerank reproduction ties Cohere Pro (nDCG 0.692 vs 0.691, CI crosses 0) with 24.7% order sensitivity (intake-1486); the vendor's own evals put the system behind Sol/Opus (intake-1471#record, corrected). Calibration is the named gap: 65% of a 7B's wrong fields scored >0.90 (intake-1474), so no gate precedes TD-2's ECE/reliability measurement.

### Open questions

- Which reader is cheapest *adequate* under a same-rubric local comparison, and at what agreement level does a two-reader redundancy arm actually buy human-disagreement cost reduction?
- Does TD-2's calibration transfer across schemas, or is ECE per (model, schema) the only honest unit?

## Compiled Update — 2026-09-17 (evening): cited evidence must resolve on main, and a port can drop the fix that produced its evidence

**Confidence: verified**: `git cherry` and blob comparisons recorded by the landing audit.

### Key findings

- **"Not on origin" meant "not on origin/main".** The 2026-09-16 preservation pass found that all three
  commits the research shared clone was "ahead" by already existed on pushed branches. The closure audit's
  wording had conflated the two. What matters for a citation is whether it resolves on main.
  [sub-preserve](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-16-sub-preserve.md),
  [sub-closure-fix](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-16-sub-closure-fix.md)
- **The landing audit reconciled the evidence.**
  - Each 2026-09-16 research commit was classified as LANDED, LANDED-DIFFERENT, MISSING or declined.
  - Every class with a counterpart on main was annotated in place: 43 "(on main as `<sha>`)" notes across
    18 files, with history text unchanged.
  - The CJ-1e GPQA pair summaries (`c72e5ad2`) are on main at `8146880b`, 6/6 identical. The §5 MoE
    pre-registration is on main at `6cbdd856`, byte-identical to the run's scratch copy.
  - [sub-land-evidence](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-17-sub-land-evidence.md),
    [canonical-judge-suite-revamp](../handoffs/active/canonical-judge-suite-revamp.md)
- **A newer port is not necessarily a superset.** Main's INF-61 driver (`a280853d`) was newer than the branch
  version, but it lacked the `taskset -apc` fence that produced the evidence. Main therefore could not
  reproduce its own result until `c6e63877` ported the fence.
  [sub-land-evidence](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-17-sub-land-evidence.md)
- **Where each landing commit lives.** A merge that would also have published `1780fa7b` (the INF-70
  evidence) was avoided on 2026-09-16. The commit is now on research main as `56ef1404`.
  [sub-integrate](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-16-sub-integrate.md)

## Compiled Update — 2026-09-17 (later): a harness-null-pool result is evidence about the SPREAD, and a promotion verdict must record the rule it used

**Confidence: external** for every number in the first four findings — they are dive-verified intake entries that motivate a filed evidence packet and gate no local decision (MEASUREMENT.md). **Verified** for the promotion-rule recording and the PRB-T4 landing, which are code facts.

This is the same family the 2026-09-14 harness-search section opened: an aggregate "harness win" says something about the regime and the spread before it says anything about a harness. The Dream-RSI intake batch (intake-1435…1459) added the strongest external instance of the null half, and the AutoPilot gate work added a field the journal was missing.

### Key findings

- **30 budget-matched harnesses, 3.1M rollouts, no universally superior harness (intake-1444).** The external study's own summary statistic is a checkpoint-pruned portfolio at 85.75% against 82.49% for the best fixed choice, i.e. the gain comes from selecting per checkpoint, not from any one harness winning everywhere. Filed as HS-E1 into the HS-4 evidence base as context only; HS-4 selection stays operator-owned and nothing in the packet gates it. ([harness-selection-and-integration](../handoffs/active/harness-selection-and-integration.md), [research-intake-dreamrsi](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-16-research-intake-dreamrsi.md))
- **An independent 12-task convergence and a fluid-bandit portfolio (intake-1457).** A second study reports no universally best harness on its 12-task set, and a fluid bandit at 0.780 against 0.718 for its comparator. Same evidentiary role: external motivation for SEQ-4's offline allocation replay and for the AC-CORAL portfolio spike, not a result to quote as ours. ([autopilot-sequential-allocation](../handoffs/active/autopilot-sequential-allocation.md), [agent-collab-rnd-harness](../handoffs/active/agent-collab-rnd-harness.md))
- **A swarm-search artifact has two confounds before it has a finding (intake-1450, intake-1451).** The SwarmResearch reproduction count (13/15) is **threshold-ambiguous and therefore unverifiable as stated** — a derived rate is not a measured one. The EvoReplay study's line-level cycling rate (~30%) and its BO tuning ceiling (13/15) come with a **knob-exposure confound**: how much of the search space was actually reachable is a property of the harness, not the search policy. The lineage adapter's first job is to report the cycling rate and the per-edit-type yield beside the exposure, not to adopt the constants. ([autokernel-research-loop](../handoffs/active/autokernel-research-loop.md))
- **Dream-RSI's own record needed four corrections, and they are the measurement content of its intake.** The implemented replay reward is App. B.2's `pareto.auc − λ·parallel_penalty`, **not the paper's Eq. 1**; the `1.22× / 1.74× / zero-gradient` claims live only in the companion README; EvoX scores strategies online only, so it has no replay number to import; PACEvolve's MBB/CE mechanisms require a known target bound (kernels have none); and SimpleTES's "generations" is Dream-RSI's relabeling of evaluator queries. Cite the corrected form or not at all. ([research-intake-dreamrsi](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-16-research-intake-dreamrsi.md), [autokernel-research-loop](../handoffs/active/autokernel-research-loop.md))
- **The promotion verdict now records which RULE produced it.** `promotion_rule` (`frontier` / `empty_frontier_repro` / `seed`) is written to the trial row and the `baseline_promotion` event, alongside `eval_details.frontier_admission = "representative"` on clean rows — the field exists because the same promotion can come from three different admission rules and a bare pass/fail hides which one. VB-AP-PROMO-RULE files the projection of both fields into the belief-kernel support frame as an additive change with no grade change. ([objective-task-rate-goodput](../handoffs/active/objective-task-rate-goodput.md), [vidya-belief-substrate-program](../handoffs/active/vidya-belief-substrate-program.md))
- **The PRB-T4 scorer fix now exists on orchestrator main.** `f0015306` (the stale live pool, the A–H multiple-choice scorer and the first-n loader fixes described in the section below) landed on orchestrator `main` as of 2026-09-17; the previous note that landing was blocked by the held orchestrator push lock is superseded. The live-pool swap and the mmlu_pro re-stamp still need the tower owner or inference. ([per-request-reasoning-budget](../handoffs/active/per-request-reasoning-budget.md))
- **The v7 runner pins were re-sealed by an operator-gated ratify run, and the package discloses exactly what the new seal crosses.** The package (`sub/v7-repin-20260916` `fc6610b9`, built off research `e4576171`) updates `dflash2_followups.py`'s `EXPECTED.runner_sha256` and the P3 bakeoff manifest's two runner pins; because the eval tower is a §5 trust boundary, the apply script shows a dry-run, requires the operator to type `RESEAL`, commits exactly six paths and never pushes. The 2026-09-17 wrap-up records the apply as **RATIFIED** at research `6d010a92`. The decision text names only `79721927`, but the re-seal also crosses `baf36757` (via `6dea92dd`) — an **additive** change that alters no request byte and no scoring decision, adding two output keys (`effective_request`, `meta.sampling_fields_are_requested_not_effective`); no P3 capture exists, so nothing banked is re-labelled. Zero-inference stub-server equivalence evidence: request bytes identical in 6/6 scenarios, outputs identical modulo the two added keys, and negative controls detect a request mutant and a scoring mutant. `critic_tasks_v1.json` (LiveCodeBench text, redistribution licence unclear) deliberately stays out of git. ([sub-v7-repin](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-16-sub-v7-repin.md), [main-handoff-sweep](../progress/2026-09/2026-09-17-main-handoff-sweep.md))

### Open questions

- HS-E1's four external items are filed as evidence rows; the packet is not yet in the HS-4 decision doc's evidence table.
- Do the checkpoint-pruned-portfolio and fluid-bandit gains survive a budget-matched replay on OUR stored search histories (SEQ-4, AK-adaptive panel), or do they collapse like the August harness-search candidates did within regime?
